How to interpret this map
  • Curriculum nodes are ABPath-approved tags with visible record counts. Only tags that passed the local visibility gate appear here.
  • High-yield sections show representative tags for major organ systems (Ovary, Prostate, Breast, GI, Lung, Derm, Bone, Soft tissue, Cyto).
  • Review queue (4,245) holds PathOut and WHO tags awaiting manual review — they are not shown as curriculum nodes.
  • Rejected / hidden (36,284) includes non-matching textbook/lecture tags, forbidden patterns, and generated junk — intentionally excluded from this view.
  • Forbidden visible tags = 0 confirms no lecture, textbook, slide, or error patterns leaked into the visible curriculum.

The review queue and rejected counts are expected governance outcomes, not errors. They reflect tags held back until reviewed or matched to ABPath. Full lists live in review_queue_v0_2.csv and rejected_tags_v0_2.csv.
